// ============================================================================
// Precomputation Phase
// ============================================================================

void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");

    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
    // At least four complete rows per worker amortizes creation and scheduling
    // on small meshes; larger meshes still expand to the full OpenMP team.
    const int kijThreads = threadCountForWork(n, n, 4);
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 1) num_threads(kijThreads)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        // A row-local generator makes the output reproducible regardless of
        // the OpenMP thread count or dynamic scheduling order.
        RandomGenerator rng(randomSeedForRow(i));
        const size_t row = i * n;
        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
            if (i == j) continue;
            state.kij[row + j] = computeKij(
                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
        }
    }
    printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", n, n);
}

void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\n");

    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
    std::vector<Vec3> centers(n);
    const int centerThreads = threadCountForWork(n, n, 1024);
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(centerThreads)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        centers[i] = state.triangles[i].center();
    }

    const size_t pairCount = n > 1 ? n * (n - 1) / 2 : 0;
    const int tauThreads = threadCountForWork(n, pairCount, 16384);
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(tauThreads)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        const size_t rowI = i * n;
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const int delay = static_cast<int>(
                std::ceil((centers[i] - centers[j]).norm() * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
            state.tau[rowI + j] = delay;
            state.tau[j * n + i] = delay;
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
// ============================================================================

void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");

    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
    const size_t numTimesteps = state.numTimesteps;
    const int simulationThreads = threadCountForWork(n, n * n, 16384);
    const val_t* const areas = state.areas.data();
    const val_t* const rho = state.rho.data();
    const val_t* const kij = state.kij.data();
    const int* const tau = state.tau.data();
    const val_t* const radE = state.radE.data();
    val_t* const radB = state.radB.data();

    // Keep one team alive across all timesteps.  The implicit barrier on the
    // worksharing loop is the wavefront boundary: all values at t are complete
    // before any thread advances to t + 1.
    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(simulationThreads)
    {
        for (size_t t = 0; t < numTimesteps; ++t) {
            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
                val_t sumB = ZERO;
                const size_t row = i * n;

                for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
                    if (i == j) continue;

                    const int tauij = tau[row + j];

                    // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i
                    if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;

                    const val_t formFactor = kij[row + j];
                    if (formFactor <= ZERO) continue;

                    // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred
                    const size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);
                    const val_t radJ = radB[srcTime * n + j];
                    if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;

                    // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity
                    sumB += std::min(formFactor * areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
                }

                // Update radiosity: reflection + emission
                radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[t * n + i];
            }

            #pragma omp single nowait
            if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == numTimesteps) {
                printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, numTimesteps);
            }
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
// ============================================================================

void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");

    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;
    const size_t numTimesteps = state.numTimesteps;

    // The simulation is time-major, which is ideal for propagation.  Make a
    // temporary triangle-major view so each correlation walks contiguous data.
    std::vector<val_t> histories(n * numTimesteps);
    const int transposeThreads = threadCountForWork(n, n * numTimesteps, 4096);
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(transposeThreads)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        val_t* const history = histories.data() + i * numTimesteps;
        for (size_t t = 0; t < numTimesteps; ++t) {
            history[t] = state.radB[t * n + i];
        }
    }

    const val_t* const sourceHistory =
        histories.data() + state.sourceIndex * numTimesteps;
    const int distanceThreads = threadCountForWork(
        n, n * numTimesteps * numTimesteps, 32768);
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(distanceThreads)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
        int bestT = 0;
        const val_t* const history = histories.data() + i * numTimesteps;

        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay
        for (size_t t = 0; t < numTimesteps; ++t) {
            val_t sum = ZERO;

            for (size_t tt = t; tt < numTimesteps; ++tt) {
    return subdivisions;
}

// ============================================================================
// Main
// ============================================================================

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\n");
    printf("               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\n");
    printf("               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\n");
    printf("  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\n");
    printf("  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -o           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    // Team sizes are selected explicitly for each kernel below.
    omp_set_dynamic(0);

    int targetTriangles = 320;
    int timesteps = 50;
    int sourceIdx = 0;
    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;

    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-o") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }

    int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);

    printf("Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n");
    printf("===================================\n");
    printf("Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\n", targetTriangles, subdivisions);

+// Give each form-factor row its own deterministic stream.  This makes rows
+// independent (and therefore safe to schedule in any order) while retaining
+// the same uniform sampling used by the sequential implementation.
+uint32_t randomSeedForRow(size_t row) {
+    return 42u + static_cast<uint32_t>(row) * 0x9e3779b9u;
+}
+
+// Avoid teams that are larger than the available independent work.  In the
+// timestep loop, also keep enough work per thread to amortize each barrier.
+int threadCountForWork(size_t independentItems, size_t totalWork,
+                       size_t minWorkPerThread) {
+    if (independentItems == 0 || totalWork == 0) return 1;
+
+    const size_t workLimitedThreads =
+        (totalWork + minWorkPerThread - 1) / minWorkPerThread;
+    const size_t usefulThreads = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min(independentItems,
+                                                              workLimitedThreads));
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
+                                             usefulThreads));
+}

+// Give each form-factor row its own deterministic stream.  This makes rows
+// independent (and therefore safe to schedule in any order) while retaining
+// the same uniform sampling used by the sequential implementation.
+uint32_t randomSeedForRow(size_t row) {
+    return 42u + static_cast<uint32_t>(row) * 0x9e3779b9u;
+}
+
+// Avoid teams that are larger than the available independent work.  In the
+// timestep loop, also keep enough work per thread to amortize each barrier.
+int threadCountForWork(size_t independentItems, size_t totalWork,
+                       size_t minWorkPerThread) {
+    if (independentItems == 0 || totalWork == 0) return 1;
+
+    const size_t workLimitedThreads =
+        (totalWork + minWorkPerThread - 1) / minWorkPerThread;
+    const size_t usefulThreads = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min(independentItems,
+                                                              workLimitedThreads));
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
+                                             usefulThreads));
+}
+

+// Give each form-factor row its own deterministic stream.  This makes rows
+// independent (and therefore safe to schedule in any order) while retaining
+// the same uniform sampling used by the sequential implementation.
+uint32_t randomSeedForRow(size_t row) {
+    return 42u + static_cast<uint32_t>(row) * 0x9e3779b9u;
+}
+
+// Avoid teams that are larger than the available independent work.  In the
+// timestep loop, also keep enough work per thread to amortize each barrier.
+int threadCountForWork(size_t independentItems, size_t totalWork,
+                       size_t minWorkPerThread) {
+    if (independentItems == 0 || totalWork == 0) return 1;
+
+    const size_t workLimitedThreads =
+        (totalWork + minWorkPerThread - 1) / minWorkPerThread;
+    const size_t usefulThreads = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min(independentItems,
+                                                              workLimitedThreads));
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
+                                             usefulThreads));
+}
+

+// Give each form-factor row its own deterministic stream.  This makes rows
+// independent (and therefore safe to schedule in any order) while retaining
+// the same uniform sampling used by the sequential implementation.
+uint32_t randomSeedForRow(size_t row) {
+    return 42u + static_cast<uint32_t>(row) * 0x9e3779b9u;
+}
+
+// Avoid teams that are larger than the available independent work.  In the
+// timestep loop, also keep enough work per thread to amortize each barrier.
+int threadCountForWork(size_t independentItems, size_t totalWork,
+                       size_t minWorkPerThread) {
+    if (independentItems == 0 || totalWork == 0) return 1;
+
+    const size_t workLimitedThreads =
+        (totalWork + minWorkPerThread - 1) / minWorkPerThread;
+    const size_t usefulThreads = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min(independentItems,
+                                                              workLimitedThreads));
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
+                                             usefulThreads));
+}
+

+// Give each form-factor row its own deterministic stream.  This makes rows
+// independent (and therefore safe to schedule in any order) while retaining
+// the same uniform sampling used by the sequential implementation.
+uint32_t randomSeedForRow(size_t row) {
+    return 42u + static_cast<uint32_t>(row) * 0x9e3779b9u;
+}
+
+// Avoid teams that are larger than the available independent work.  In the
+// timestep loop, also keep enough work per thread to amortize each barrier.
+int threadCountForWork(size_t independentItems, size_t totalWork,
+                       size_t minWorkPerThread) {
+    if (independentItems == 0 || totalWork == 0) return 1;
+
+    const size_t workLimitedThreads =
+        1 + (totalWork - 1) / minWorkPerThread;
+    const size_t usefulThreads = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min(independentItems,
+                                                              workLimitedThreads));
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
+                                             usefulThreads));
+}
+

+    const int centerThreads = threadCountForWork(n, n, 1024);
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(centerThreads)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        centers[i] = state.triangles[i].center();
+    }

+    const size_t pairCount = n > 1 ? n * (n - 1) / 2 : 0;

+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(guided) num_threads(tauThreads)

+        const size_t rowI = i * n;
+        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
+            const int delay = computeTau(centers[i], centers[j]);
+            state.tau[rowI + j] = delay;
+            state.tau[j * n + i] = delay;

+// Give each form-factor row its own deterministic stream.  This makes rows
+// independent (and therefore safe to schedule in any order) while retaining
+// the same uniform sampling used by the sequential implementation.
+uint32_t randomSeedForRow(size_t row) {
+    return 42u + static_cast<uint32_t>(row) * 0x9e3779b9u;
+}
+
+// Avoid teams that are larger than the available independent work.  In the
+// timestep loop, also keep enough work per thread to amortize each barrier.
+int threadCountForWork(size_t independentItems, size_t totalWork,
+                       size_t minWorkPerThread) {
+    if (independentItems == 0 || totalWork == 0) return 1;
+
+    const size_t workLimitedThreads =
+        1 + (totalWork - 1) / minWorkPerThread;
+    const size_t usefulThreads = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min(independentItems,
+                                                              workLimitedThreads));
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
+                                             usefulThreads));
+}

+    const int centerThreads = threadCountForWork(n, n, 1024);
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(centerThreads)

+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(guided) num_threads(tauThreads)

+            state.tau[j * n + i] = delay;

Key improvements include parallel form-factor and delay computation, persistent parallel wave propagation, cache-local parallel cross-correlation, deterministic thread-independent random sampling, and workload-aware team sizing. OpenMP is now required unconditionally.

     val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }

+// Give each form-factor row its own deterministic stream.  This makes rows
+// independent (and therefore safe to schedule in any order) while retaining
+// the same uniform sampling used by the sequential implementation.
+uint32_t randomSeedForRow(size_t row) {
+    return 42u + static_cast<uint32_t>(row) * 0x9e3779b9u;
+}
+
+// Avoid teams that are larger than the available independent work.  In the
+// timestep loop, also keep enough work per thread to amortize each barrier.
+int threadCountForWork(size_t independentItems, size_t totalWork,
+                       size_t minWorkPerThread) {
+    if (independentItems == 0 || totalWork == 0) return 1;
+
+    const size_t workLimitedThreads =
+        1 + (totalWork - 1) / minWorkPerThread;
+    const size_t usefulThreads = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min(independentItems,
+                                                              workLimitedThreads));
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
+                                             usefulThreads));
+}
+
